We focus on larger models to understand their behaviour. Moreover, vanilla transformers are still de facto a standard in other domains, while conformers were adopted only in speech recognition. Therefore, focusing on vanilla transformer models will broaden the impact of our findings for speech recognition on the FL and DP communities at large.

### D.6 Seed Models

gao2022e2easr trained seed models to initialize FL using a small fraction of speakers (117 speakers, or 2.8%, for French and 99 speakers, or 13.2%, for Italian) and used the rest of the data for FL training. Recent work berrebbi2023more showed that model quality depends on the number of speakers and the diversity of the training data: it is better to have more speakers with shorter total audio duration than to have fewer speakers with longer total audio duration.

Based on the recommendation of berrebbi2023more to have at least 1k speakers in the training data, we randomly sampled $`5\%`$ (English) or $`10\%`$ (all languages) of speakers for the in-domain seed model training. This provided more than 1k users for training CV seed models for English. While for French the seed model is trained from only $`685`$ users and for German the seed model is trained from only $`712`$ users, we note that French and German languages are easier to train. Furthermore, for FL models training on CV (English) we use a seed model trained on LS-100 that has only 251 speakers; however, LS-100 has over 100 hours of audio, which is approximately $`6.3\%`$ of the total audio in CV.

Preliminary experiments showed that the seed model training on a subset of $`5\%`$ speakers with the shortest total audio does not converge: even for English the subset contains less than 2 hours of audio, which is known to be hard for any E2E ASR model training. In contrast, if we take a subset of $`5\%`$ speakers with the longest total audio as in gao2022e2easr, a seed model is very well trained as then the dataset has more than $`64\%`$ of total audio in the CV dataset for English language and training on the rest of the data brings little benefit. Thus, we found the subsets with minimum-duration or maximum-duration users to not be practical scenarios.

For LS, validation (test) set has 5h of audio with mean of $`\sim`$8min and standard deviation of 0.1min for the total duration per speaker. For CV, validation (test) set has $`\sim`$30h with mean of $`\sim`$15s and standard deviation of 1.5s for the total duration per speaker. Thus validation and test datasets have homogeneous distribution which weights speakers (users) equally for evaluation. For both LS and CV we use original validation and test sets, without any modification. Thus, the disjoint set of speakers in different splits and the disjoint set of speakers in a seed model and FL training ensure that speakers (clients) are not accounted twice in the privacy budget.

### D.7 Limitations

Our theoretical results are derived under some assumptions listed in Section [3](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#S3 "3 Theoretical Analysis: Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"). Empirical results are limited to i) LibriSpeech and CommonVoice (en, de, fr) read speech data; ii) monolingual models; iii) CTC-based models of size 100M-500M parameters; iv) absence of external language models; v) audio data assumed to be labeled. Future work would include theoretical and empirical analysis to overcome these limitations.

## Appendix E Theoretical Analysis

### E.1 Assumptions

Given a global model comprising of $`{H}`$ layers, the model parameters are defined as $`\boldsymbol{\theta}=(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{1},\cdots,\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h},\cdots\boldsymbol{\theta}_{H})`$. It is presumed that the loss function for each sample $`\boldsymbol{x}`$ is bounded below: $`\min_{{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{{D}}}\ell(\boldsymbol{x},{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})>-\infty`$, where $`\boldsymbol{x}\sim\mathcal{D}_{k}`$, $`\forall\,{k}`$. Let $`\|\cdot\|`$ denote the $`l_{2}`$-norm. Our analysis uses the following standard assumptions (wang2020tackling; reddi2021adaptive; friedlander2012hybrid; hosseinalipour2020multi; li2019convergence; stich2018local; azam2022lbgm):

1.  1.
    Smoothness of Gradient of Loss Function: Gradient of loss function is layer-wise $`{L}_{h}`$-smooth for $`\forall h`$ (you2020lamb):

    ``` math
    \left\|{\nabla}_{h}\ell(\boldsymbol{x},{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})-{\nabla}_{h}\ell(\boldsymbol{x},{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{\prime}})\right\|\leq{L}_{h}\left\|{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}-{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{\prime}}\right\|,\\
    \penalty 10000\ \penalty 10000\ \forall\,{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{\prime}}\in\mathbb{R}^{{D}},\,\boldsymbol{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{N},\forall k,              
    ```
    where $`{\nabla}_{h}`$ denotes gradient with respect to parameters $`\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}`$ of layer $`{h}`$. Consequently, the loss function is also $`{L}`$-smooth, where $`{L}=\left\|\left({L}_{1},\cdots,{L}_{H}\right)\right\|_{2}`$:

    ``` math
    \left\|{\nabla}\ell(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta})-{\nabla}\ell(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\prime})\right\|\leq L\left\|\boldsymbol{\theta}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\prime}\right\|,\\
    \penalty 10000\ \penalty 10000\ \forall\,\boldsymbol{\theta},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{D},\boldsymbol{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{N},\forall k.
    ```
2.  2.
    Local Gradient Characteristics: Given user $`k`$, $`\mathscr{B}_{k}=\left\{\boldsymbol{x}_{i}\right\}_{i=1}^{B},\boldsymbol{x}_{i}\sim\mathcal{D}_{k}`$ and local gradient $`\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta})`$ for $`\boldsymbol{x}\sim\mathcal{D}_{k}`$, its estimator $`{\mathbf{g}}_{{k}}(\boldsymbol{\theta})={\mathbf{g}}_{{k}}(\mathscr{B}_{k},\boldsymbol{\theta})`$ (e.g. obtained by SGD) is an unbiased estimator and have a bounded variance (wang2020tackling; friedlander2012hybrid; azam2022lbgm), thus:

    $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{}_{\mathscr{B}_{k}}}\left[\,{\mathbf{g}}_{{k}}(\boldsymbol{\theta})\,\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\nabla\mathscr{L}_{k}(\boldsymbol{\theta})\text{, and }`$$`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{}_{\mathscr{B}_{k}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{{k}}({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})-\nabla\mathscr{L}_{k}({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2},\,\,\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\geq 0,\penalty 10000\ \penalty 10000\ \forall\,{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{{D}},\penalty 10000\ \penalty 10000\ \forall k.`$
3.  3.
    Global Pseudo-Gradient Characteristics: The variance of global (pseudo-) gradient is assumed to be bounded (li2019convergence; reddi2021adaptive) such that:

    ``` math
    \sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\left\|\nabla\mathscr{L}_{k}(\boldsymbol{\theta})-\nabla\mathscr{L}(\boldsymbol{\theta})\right\|^{2}\leq\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2},\penalty 10000\ \penalty 10000\ \sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}\geq 0,\penalty 10000\ \penalty 10000\ \forall\,\boldsymbol{\theta}\in\mathbb{R}^{{D}}.
    ```

    To give a probabilistic interpretation of this assumption we can estimate the global loss gradient $`\nabla\mathscr{L}(\boldsymbol{\theta})`$ by sampling one user $`u\sim\mathrm{Categorical}(\omega_{1},\dots,\omega_{K})`$ and using the following unbiased estimator $`\nabla\hat{\mathscr{L}}(u,\boldsymbol{\theta})=\nabla\mathscr{L}_{u}(\boldsymbol{\theta})`$. Then,

    ``` math
    \mathbb{E}_{{}_{u\sim\mathrm{Categorical}(\omega_{1},\dots,\omega_{K})}}\left[\nabla\hat{\mathscr{L}}(u,\boldsymbol{\theta})\right]=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}[u=k]\nabla\mathscr{L}_{k}(\boldsymbol{\theta})=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{k}\nabla\mathscr{L}_{k}(\boldsymbol{\theta})=\nabla\mathscr{L}(\boldsymbol{\theta}).
    ```
    From the latter we get the variance of the estimator and extend it to the left-hand side of Equation [A3](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex5 "Equation A3 ‣ Item 3 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"). Thus, we can interpret this assumption as the variance of global (pseudo-) gradient.

### E.2 DP Assumptions

To incorporate user-level DP into FL, we consider that every client is sampled i.i.d. with probability $`q`$ ($`qK=S`$) and then the client updates $`\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{k}^{(t)}`$ are: (i) clipped such that their $`l_{2}`$ norm is bounded, i.e., $`\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{k}^{(t)}\|_{2}\leq C`$ at every central training step $`t`$ and then (ii) perturbed via Gaussian mechanism, such that final client updates under FL with DP are given by $`{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{k}}^{{(t)}}+\mathcal{N}\left(0,\boldsymbol{I}C^{2}\sigma_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}\frac{q}{\sum_{i=1}^{K}\omega_{i}^{2}}\right)`$, where $`{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{k}}^{{(t)}}=\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}{\alpha}_{k}^{(t)}{\mathbf{G}}_{{k}}^{{(t)}}`$ and $`{\alpha}_{k}^{(t)}=\frac{C}{\mathrm{max}\left(C,\|\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{k}}^{{(t)}}\|\right)}`$. For $`\sum_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{k}=1`$, where $`\omega_{k}\in(0,1)`$, we can extend Theorem [1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#Thmtheorem1 "Theorem 1. ‣ FL with Differential Privacy (DP) ‣ 2 Federated Learning with Differential Privacy: Background and Notation ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") to the weighted loss case by defining sensitivity $`\mathbb{S}=\mathrm{max}_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{k}/q`$ per Lemma 1 from mcmahan2018learning. Having $`\omega_{k}=1/K`$, we get exactly sensitivity definition $`\mathbb{S}=1/(qK)`$ from Theorem [1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#Thmtheorem1 "Theorem 1. ‣ FL with Differential Privacy (DP) ‣ 2 Federated Learning with Differential Privacy: Background and Notation ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping").

### E.3 Helpful Lemmas

###### Lemma 1.

For any positive variables $`{C},{X},{Y}\in\mathbb{R}^{+}`$, we have

``` math
\frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{X}\right)}-\frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{Y}\right)}\leq\frac{\left|{X}-{Y}\right|}{{C}^{2}}
```

###### Proof.

We can prove it by analyzing three independent cases:

1.  (i)
    if $`{C}\geq{X}`$ and $`{C}\geq{Y}`$ we trivially have

    ``` math
    \frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{X}\right)}-\frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{Y}\right)}=\frac{1}{{C}}-\frac{1}{{C}}=0\leq\frac{\left|{X}-{Y}\right|}{{C}^{2}},
    ```
2.  (ii)
    if $`{C}<{X}`$ and $`{C}<{Y}`$ we have

    ``` math
    \frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{X}\right)}-\frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{Y}\right)}=\frac{1}{{X}}-\frac{1}{{Y}}\leq\frac{\left|{X}-{Y}\right|}{{X}{Y}}\leq\frac{\left|{X}-{Y}\right|}{{C}^{2}},\,\text{and}
    ```
3.  (iii)
    if $`{Y}<{C}<{X}`$ (equivalently the case $`{Y}>{C}>{X}`$) we have

    ``` math
    \frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{X}\right)}-\frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{Y}\right)}=\frac{1}{{X}}-\frac{1}{{C}}\leq\frac{\left|{X}-{C}\right|}{{X}{C}}\leq\frac{\left|{X}-{Y}\right|}{{C}^{2}}.
    ```

Thus, we can conclude $`\forall\,{C},{X},{Y}\in\mathbb{R}^{+}`$

``` math
\frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{X}\right)}-\frac{1}{\mathrm{max}\left({C},{Y}\right)}\leq\frac{\left|{X}-{Y}\right|}{{C}^{2}}.
```

∎

###### Lemma 2.

For $`{X}\in\mathbb{R}`$ and a constant $`{C}>0`$,

``` math
\left({X}-{C}\right)_{+}\leq\frac{{X}^{2}}{2{C}}
```

where $`\left({X}-{C}\right)_{+}=\mathrm{max}\left(0,{X}-{C}\right)`$.

###### Proof.

For $`{X}\leq C`$, $`\left({X}-{C}\right)_{+}\leq 0`$, and inequality holds trivially. For $`{X}>C`$, we can use the algebraic identity:

``` math
{X}^{2}\geq 2{C}\left({X}-{C}\right)
```

which can be rewritten as $`\left({X}-{C}\right)_{+}\leq\frac{{X}^{2}}{{2C}}`$. ∎

###### Lemma 3.

For a random vector $`{{\mathbf{G}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}`$ with bounded norm $`\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\leq U`$ and a clipping constant $`C>0`$, define the clipped vector as $`{\mathbf{G}}_{{C}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}`$ such that $`{\mathbf{G}}_{{C}}={\mathbf{G}}\cdot\frac{C}{\mathrm{max}\left(C,\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]\right)}`$. Then the squared distance between $`{\mathbf{G}}`$ and $`{\mathbf{G}}_{{C}}`$ is upper bounded by

``` math
\left\|{\mathbf{G}}-{\mathbf{G}}_{{C}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{U^{4}}{4{C}^{2}}.
```

###### Proof.

For $`\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]\leq C`$, we have

``` math
\left\|{\mathbf{G}}-{\mathbf{G}}_{{C}}\right\|^{2}=\left\|{\mathbf{G}}-{\mathbf{G}}\cdot\frac{C}{\mathrm{max}\left(C,\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]\right)}\right\|^{2}=\left\|{\mathbf{G}}-{\mathbf{G}}\cdot\frac{C}{C}\right\|^{2}=0.
```

For $`\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]>C`$, we can use the algebraic identity:

$`\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{G}}-{\mathbf{G}}_{{C}}\right\|^{2}`$$`\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbf{G}}-{\mathbf{G}}\cdot\frac{C}{\mathrm{max}\left(C,\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]\right)}\right\|^{2}=\left\|{\mathbf{G}}-{\mathbf{G}}\cdot\frac{C}{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]}\right\|^{2}`$$`\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{C}{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]}\right)^{2}\cdot\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|^{2}=\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]-C}{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]}\right)^{2}\cdot\left\|{\bm{G}}\right\|^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\hskip-9.10486pt\overset{\text{Lemma\penalty 10000\ \ref{lemma:scalar_upper_bound}}}{\leq}\frac{\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]\right)^{4}}{4C^{2}}\frac{\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|^{2}}{\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}\right\|\right]\right)^{2}}\leq\frac{U^{4}}{4C^{2}}.`$

Thus, the trivial case in Equation [E.5](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E5 "Equation E.5 ‣ E.3 Helpful Lemmas ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") together with the inequality in Equation [E.6](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E6 "Equation E.6 ‣ E.3 Helpful Lemmas ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") results in the final bound. ∎

### E.4 LAMB

The per-layer update rule of $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ is given by:

$`\displaystyle\footnotesize{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t+1)}}\leftarrow{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}-{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}\frac{\phi\left(\|{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\|\right)}{\|{\mathbf{u}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}+\lambda{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\|}\left({\mathbf{u}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}+\lambda{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\,\text{where}\,\lambda\geq 0,\left[{\mathbf{u}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}=\frac{\left[{\mathbf{m}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}}{\left[\sqrt{{\mathbf{v}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}}+\xi\right]_{i}},`$$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{m}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}=\beta_{1}{\mathbf{m}}_{{h}}^{{(t-1)}}+(1-\beta_{1}){\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}},{\mathbf{v}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}=\beta_{2}{\mathbf{v}}_{{h}}^{{(t-1)}}+(1-\beta_{2})\left[{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]^{2},\,\,0\leq\beta_{1},\beta_{2}\leq 1.`$

$`\phi:\mathbb{R}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}`$ is a scaling function which is often defined as an identity in standard $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ applications (you2020lamb; fong2020improving). While $`\xi`$ is a constant generally employed for numerical stability, azam2023fl_asr show that $`\xi=0.01`$ leads to best results in FL, likely because it counteracts spurious pseudo-gradients early in the training. Let’s define the trust ratio of LAMB:

``` math
{r}_{h}^{(t)}\triangleq\frac{\phi\left(\|{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\|\right)}{\|{\mathbf{u}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\|}\in\mathbb{R}\qquad\text{ and }\qquad\left[{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}\triangleq\frac{r_{h}^{(t)}}{\left[\sqrt{{\mathbf{v}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}}+\xi\right]_{i}}.
```

### E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof

###### Theorem 2.

Assume [A1.1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex1 "Equation A1.1 ‣ Item 1 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), [A2.1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex3 "Equation A2.1 ‣ Item 2 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), [A2.2](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex4 "Equation A2.2 ‣ Item 2 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), and [A3](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex5 "Equation A3 ‣ Item 3 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), $`{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}L<1`$ and $`\kappa=\left[1-8(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\right]>0`$. If the trust ratio from Eq. [E.9](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E9 "Equation E.9 ‣ E.4 LAMB ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") in $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ optimizer is controlled in the Algorithm [1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#alg1 "Algorithm 1 ‣ FL with Differential Privacy (DP) ‣ 2 Federated Learning with Differential Privacy: Background and Notation ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") (global optimizer is $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ and local optimizer is SGD) such that $`{r}_{h}^{(t)}\leq{R}_{h}`$ and $`\left\|{\mathbf{1}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|_{\infty}\leq{P}_{h}`$, $`\beta_{1}=0`$ and $`\lambda=0`$ in $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ optimizer, and clients are i.i.d. sampled with probability $`q=1`$ (no sampling), then after $`{T}`$ steps of aggregation the performance of FL with DP, per-layer clipping and layer-wise gradient normalization is characterized by the following upper bound:

$`\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{2\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(0)}}\right)-{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{\star}}\right)\right]}{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}{T}}+16H\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+32H\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{R}_{h}^{2}{d}_{h}+2\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}M_{h}^{2}\left[16L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{C_{h}^{2}}+4P_{h}^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\frac{\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}}{\xi^{2}T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{R_{h}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left[\mathbb{E}_{{}_{k}}\left[\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)\right]+\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{k}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)\right].`$

where $`k\sim\mathrm{Categorical}(\omega_{1},\dots,\omega_{K})`$ and $`\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\cdot\right]`$ denotes the expectation over sampled mini-batch $`\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}`$ every local step $`t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=1,\dots,T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}`$ from the client data: $`\boldsymbol{x}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{k}\sim\mathcal{D}_{k}`$, $`\boldsymbol{x}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{k}\in\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}`$, $`|\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}|=B_{k}`$.

###### Proof.

We assume $`\beta_{1}=0`$ and regularization $`\lambda=0`$. Then the update rule for $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ as the global optimizer at the FL server given by Equation [E.7](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E7 "Equation E.7 ‣ E.4 LAMB ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") can be rewritten:

``` math
{\mathbf{v}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}=\beta_{2}{\mathbf{v}}_{{h}}^{{(t-1)}}+(1-\beta_{2})\left[{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]^{2},\,\,0\leq\beta_{2}\leq 1,
```
``` math
\left[{\mathbf{u}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}=\frac{\left[{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}}{\left[\sqrt{{\mathbf{v}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}}+\xi\right]_{i}},
```
``` math
{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t+1)}}\leftarrow{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}-{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}\frac{\phi\left(\|{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\|\right)}{\|{\mathbf{u}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\|}{\mathbf{u}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}},\forall h.
```

Given definition of the trust ratio in Equation [E.9](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E9 "Equation E.9 ‣ E.4 LAMB ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), the update rule can be expressed as:

``` math
{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t+1)}}\leftarrow{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}-{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}.
```

The aggregated clients updates, or pseudo-gradient, $`{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}`$ are given by (as $`q=1`$):

``` math
{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\left({\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}+{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right),
```

where $`{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ is the accumulated client update (see Algorithm [1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#alg1 "Algorithm 1 ‣ FL with Differential Privacy (DP) ‣ 2 Federated Learning with Differential Privacy: Background and Notation ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping")) and $`{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,{\mathbf{I}}_{{h}}C^{2}{\sigma}_{{}_{\mathrm{DP}}}^{2}\frac{q}{\sum_{i=1}^{K}\omega_{i}^{2}}\right)`$ is the random independent $`\mathrm{DP}`$-noise added to client updates. For each client we perform $`T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}`$ steps of SGD optimization by i) sampling a mini-batch $`\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}`$ every local step $`t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=1,\dots,T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}`$ from the client data: $`\boldsymbol{x}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{k}\sim\mathcal{D}_{k}`$, $`\boldsymbol{x}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{k}\in\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}`$, $`|\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}|=B_{k}`$; ii) performing a gradient step with a local step-size (learning rate) $`\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}>0`$ having $`\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t,0)}=\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}`$:

``` math
{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{h,k}\right)=\frac{1}{B_{k}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{x}_{k}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}\in\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\nabla\ell_{h}(\boldsymbol{x}_{k}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{h,k}),
```
``` math
\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{h,k}=\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1)}_{h,k}-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1)}}\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1)}_{h,k}\right),
```

where $`{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}`$ are unbiased estimators of clients’ gradients. Then for a given per-layer clipping constant $`{C}_{h}>0`$, the client updates and the corresponding clipping multipliers are defined as:

``` math
{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}=\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t,0)}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t,T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}={\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}
```
``` math
{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}={\alpha}_{h,k}^{(t)}\,{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\qquad\text{ with }{\alpha}_{h,k}^{(t)}=\frac{{C}_{h}}{\mathrm{max}\left({C}_{h},\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)}.
```

With triangle inequality we can upper bound the norm of a random variable $`{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ given theorem assumption that $`\nabla\ell_{h}(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta})`$ is $`L`$-Lipschitz smooth and thus $`\|\nabla\ell_{h}(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta})\|\leq M_{h}`$ (e.g. $`M_{h}=\|\nabla\ell_{h}(\boldsymbol{x}_{0},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{0})\|+L\mathrm{max}_{\boldsymbol{\theta}\in\boldsymbol{\mathrm{\Theta}}}||\boldsymbol{\theta}||`$, where $`\boldsymbol{\mathrm{\Theta}}`$ is a compact):

``` math
\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\|\leq{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\|{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\|\leq{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}{M}_{h}.
```

We next define the auxiliary terms in the context of clipping:

$`\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$$`\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\text{ with }\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}=\frac{{C}_{h}}{\mathrm{max}\left({C}_{h},\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)},`$$`\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$$`\displaystyle=\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\text{ with }\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}=\frac{{C}_{h}}{\mathrm{max}\left({C}_{h},\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}},k}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)},`$

Since gradient of loss function $`\ell(\boldsymbol{x},\theta)`$ is $`L-`$Lipschitz smooth, we get the following for any two points $`\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)}`$ and $`\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}`$:

``` math
\ell(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)})\leq\ell(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)})+\left<\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}),\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}\right>+\frac{L}{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.
```

By taking expectation over the client $`k`$ data $`\boldsymbol{x}\sim\mathcal{D}_{k}`$, for every client we can write down:

``` math
\mathscr{L}_{k}(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)})\leq\mathscr{L}_{k}(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)})+\left<\nabla\mathscr{L}_{k}(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}),\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}\right>+\frac{L}{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.
```

By multiplying with $`\omega_{k}`$, summing all inequalities across clients, and using the update rule from Equation [E.13](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E13 "Equation E.13 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), we can get:

$`\displaystyle{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t+1)}}\right)\leq{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)+\left<\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right),\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}\right>+\frac{L}{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t+1)}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}`$$`\displaystyle={\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\eta_{\mathrm{glob}}\left<\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right),{\mathbf{p}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}^{{(t)}}\right>+\frac{\eta_{\mathrm{glob}}^{2}L}{2}\left\|{\mathbf{p}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}.`$

Bounding loss $`\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t+1)}}\right)\right]`$ with $`{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$ term

Now, let’s take the expectation over the mini-batches $`\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}`$ sampling in the local SGD optimization for both sides of inequality having random variables $`{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}`$ and $`{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}`$ (for short notation we use $`\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\cdot\right]`$):

$`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t+1)}}\right)\right]\leq{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\langle\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right),{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\rangle\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}{L}}{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\quad\quad\penalty 10000\ \overset{(i)}{=}{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}}{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}}{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}}{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}{L}}{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\quad\quad\penalty 10000\ \leq{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}}{2}\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}-\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}(1-{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}{L})}{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}}{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$$`\displaystyle\quad\quad\overset{(ii)}{\leq}{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}}{2}\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}}{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}.`$

where $`(i)`$ uses $`-2\left\langle a,b\right\rangle=-\left\|a\right\|^{2}-\left\|b\right\|^{2}+\left\|a-b\right\|^{2}`$ and $`(ii)`$ uses the condition $`{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}L<1`$. We can next bound $`{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$ using Equation [E.14](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E14 "Equation E.14 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), the auxiliary terms $`\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ and $`\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ defined in Equations [E.20](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E20 "Equation E.20 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping")-[E.21](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E21 "Equation E.21 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"),

$`\displaystyle{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\Bigg[\bigg\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\bigg\|^{2}\Bigg].`$

As a reminder, Jensen’s inequality for some $`\boldsymbol{y}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{D}`$ gives us:

``` math
\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{i}\boldsymbol{y}_{k}\right\|^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{K}\omega_{i}||\boldsymbol{y}_{k}||^{2}\text{ where }\sum_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{k}=1,\,\,0\leq\omega_{k}\leq 1.
```

Helpful inequalities

Using the triangle inequality first and then applying Hölder’s inequality, we get for $`\boldsymbol{y}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{D}`$

``` math
\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\boldsymbol{y}_{k}\right\|^{2}\leq\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}||\boldsymbol{y}_{k}||\right)^{2}=\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}(||\boldsymbol{y}_{k}||\cdot 1)\right)^{2}\leq K\sum_{k=1}^{K}||\boldsymbol{y}_{k}||^{2}.
```

Also, if $`\boldsymbol{y}_{1}`$ and $`\boldsymbol{y}_{2}`$ are independent random variables and $`\mathbb{E}\left[\boldsymbol{y}_{1}\right]=0`$ then:

$`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[||\boldsymbol{y}_{1}+\boldsymbol{y}_{2}||^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[||\boldsymbol{y}_{1}||^{2}+||\boldsymbol{y}_{2}||^{2}+2<\boldsymbol{y}_{1},\boldsymbol{y}_{2}>\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[||\boldsymbol{y}_{1}||^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[||\boldsymbol{y}_{2}||^{2}\right]+2<\mathbb{E}\left[\boldsymbol{y}_{1}\right],\mathbb{E}\left[\boldsymbol{y}_{2}\right]>`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[||\boldsymbol{y}_{1}||^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[||\boldsymbol{y}_{2}||^{2}\right].`$

Let’s estimate for any random variable $`{\mathbf{y}}_{{h}}`$ the following entity having that $`{\mathbf{y}}_{{h}}`$ and $`{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}`$ are not independent variables:

``` math
\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{y}}_{{h}}\right\|^{2}\right]=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{h}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left[{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]^{2}_{i}\left[{\mathbf{y}}_{{h}}\right]^{2}_{i}\right]\leq\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{h}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[{\mathbf{y}}_{{h}}\right]^{2}_{i}=\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{y}}_{{h}}\right\|^{2}\right].
```

Bounding term with DP noise in $`{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$

Having upper bound on the expectation $`\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}^{2}\leq\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}`$ and random independent DP noise $`{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,{\mathbf{I}}_{{h}}C^{2}{\sigma}_{{}_{\mathrm{DP}}}^{2}\frac{1}{\sum_{i=1}^{K}\omega_{i}^{2}}\right)`$ as $`q=1`$ per theorem condition (thus $`{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}`$ and $`{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ are independent variables), let’s get the upper bound first for:

$`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{h}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left[{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}^{2}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right]^{2}_{i}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{h}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{i}^{2}\leq\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}d_{h}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right]_{0}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle=\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}d_{h}C^{2}{\sigma}_{{}_{\mathrm{DP}}}^{2}\frac{1}{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{k}^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}^{2}=\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}d_{h}C^{2}{\sigma}_{{}_{\mathrm{DP}}}^{2}.`$

Bounding $`{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$ with $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{1}}`$, $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{2}}`$, $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{3}}`$, $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{4}}`$ terms

Given Equations [E.28](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E28 "Equation E.28 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), [E.32](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E32 "Equation E.32 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), [E.29](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E29 "Equation E.29 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") and [E.30](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E30 "Equation E.30 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") (we use the fact that DP noise $`{\mathbf{z}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ is zero-mean independent variable), we can bound $`{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}`$ in the following way:

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}`$$`\displaystyle\leq 4\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{1}}}+4\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{2}}}`$$`\displaystyle\quad+4\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\left({\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{3}}}+4\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{4}}}`$$`\displaystyle\quad+\frac{{R}_{h}^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}{d}_{h}C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}.`$

Bounding $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{1}}`$ term

Defining $`{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}=\frac{1}{{T}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{{t}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{{T}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,{t}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}`$ and $`{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}=\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$:

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{Y}}_{{1}}`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{\text{Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:jensens}}}{\leq}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)+\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\overset{\text{Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:norm-holder}}}{\leq}2\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=2(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+2\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{X}}},`$

where

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{X}}`$$`\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)+\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:norm-holder}}}{\leq}2\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\ref{eqn:global_variance}}{\leq}2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\frac{1}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right)+\frac{1}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right)-{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:norm-holder}}}{\leq}2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+4\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\frac{1}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]`$ $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+4\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right)-\frac{1}{{T}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{{t}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{{T}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,{t}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:jensens}}}{\leq}2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+\frac{4}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{4}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right)-{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,{t}_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\ref{eqn:sgd_noise}}{\leq}2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+\frac{4}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,0)}}\right)-\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h,k}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+4\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}`$ $`\displaystyle\overset{\ref{eqn:lipschitz}}{\leq}2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+4\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+\frac{4L^{2}}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,0)}}-{\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eqn:local_update rule}}}{=}2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+4\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+\frac{4L^{2}}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\underbrace{\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\sum_{s=0}^{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,s)}}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{W}}},`$

where

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{W}}`$$`\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\sum_{s=0}^{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,s)}}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{\text{Eq. }\ref{eq:norm-holder}}{\leq}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}\sum_{s=0}^{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{g}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t,s)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\|\nabla\ell_{h}(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{\theta})\|\leq M_{h}}{\leq}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\sum_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=0}^{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}-1}t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\leq\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{3}.`$

Substituting it back in $`{\mathbf{X}}`$, we get:

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{X}}\leq 2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+4\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+\frac{4L^{2}}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{3}=2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+4\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+4L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2},`$

which we can substitute in $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{1}}`$, thus getting the bound:

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{Y}}_{{1}}`$$`\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+2\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\omega}_{k}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)-{\mathbf{H}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]}_{{\mathbf{X}}}`$$`\displaystyle\leq 2(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+2\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\left[2\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+4\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+4L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=2(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+4\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+8\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+8L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{4}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{4}M_{h}^{2}.`$

Bounding $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{2}}`$ term

We next bound $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{2}}`$ using $`{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ defined in Equation [E.17](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E17 "Equation E.17 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") and its bound defined in Equation [E.19](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E19 "Equation E.19 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"):

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{Y}}_{{2}}`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}+\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:norm-holder}}}{\leq}2\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{Lemma\penalty 10000\ \ref{lemma:clip_upper_bound} and Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:Gnorm-bound}}}{\leq}\frac{\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{2C_{h}^{2}}+2\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right].`$

Given the theorem’s $`\left\|{\mathbf{1}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|_{\infty}\leq{P}_{h}`$ assumption⁴⁴4This assumption is reasonable given $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ optimizer bounds its trust ratio., the latter term we can bound as:

$`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\left({\mathbf{1}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\odot\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{similar to Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:bound-p}}}{\leq}P_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{\text{ Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:Gnorm-bound}}}{\leq}P_{h}^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\left|\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right|^{2}\leq P_{h}^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}.`$

Substituting the latest bound back into $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{2}}`$, we finally can write:

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{Y}}_{{2}}\leq\frac{\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{2C_{h}^{2}}+2P_{h}^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}.`$

Bounding $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{3}}`$ term

We can next bound $`{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{3}}}`$ as follows:

$`\displaystyle{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{3}}}`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\left({\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{\text{ Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:bound-p}}}{\leq}\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\alpha}_{h,k}^{(t)}\,{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\,{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]=\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left({\alpha}_{h,k}^{(t)}-\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{ Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:Gnorm-bound}}}{\leq}\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left({\alpha}_{h,k}^{(t)}-\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)^{2}\right].`$

Using $`{\alpha}_{h,k}^{(t)}`$ and $`\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ defined in Equations [E.18](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E18 "Equation E.18 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") and [E.20](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E20 "Equation E.20 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), we have the following:

$`\displaystyle\left({\alpha}_{h,k}^{(t)}-\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)^{2}`$$`\displaystyle=\left(\frac{{C}_{h}}{\mathrm{max}\left({C}_{h},\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)}-\frac{{C}_{h}}{\mathrm{max}\left({C}_{h},\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)}\right)^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{Lemma\penalty 10000\ \ref{lemma:max_upper_bound}}}{\leq}\frac{\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|-\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)^{2}}{{C}_{h}^{2}}.`$

Consequently, $`{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{3}}}`$ can be bounded as

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{Y}}_{{3}}`$$`\displaystyle\leq\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left({\alpha}_{h,k}^{(t)}-\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\leq\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|-\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)^{2}\right]}{{C}_{h}^{2}}`$$`\displaystyle=\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)}{{C}_{h}^{2}}.`$

Bounding $`{\mathbf{Y}}_{{4}}`$ term

We can finally bound $`{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{4}}}`$ as follows:

$`\displaystyle{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{4}}}`$$`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\odot\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\overset{\text{ Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:bound-p}}}{\leq}\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{\mathbf{\Delta}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle=\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\,{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\,{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]=\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left(\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{ Eq.\penalty 10000\ \ref{eq:Gnorm-bound}}}{\leq}\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left(\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)^{2}\right].`$

Similarly, using $`\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ and $`\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}`$ defined in Equations [E.20](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E20 "Equation E.20 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") and [E.21](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E21 "Equation E.21 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") we have the following:

$`\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)^{2}`$$`\displaystyle=\left(\frac{{C}_{h}}{\mathrm{max}\left({C}_{h},\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)}-\frac{{C}_{h}}{\mathrm{max}\left({C}_{h},\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}},k}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)}\right)^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\overset{\text{Lemma\penalty 10000\ \ref{lemma:max_upper_bound}}}{\leq}\frac{\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]-\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}},k}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)^{2}}{{C}_{h}^{2}}.`$

We can thus bound $`{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{4}}}`$ as

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{Y}}_{{4}}`$$`\displaystyle\leq\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left(\widetilde{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}-\overline{{\alpha}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right)^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\leq\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\frac{\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]-\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}},k}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)^{2}}{{C}_{h}^{2}}.`$

Final bound on $`{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$ term

Substituting $`{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{1}}}`$, $`{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{2}}}`$, $`{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{3}}}`$, and $`{{\mathbf{Y}}_{{4}}}`$ back in $`{{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}}`$ and having $`k\sim\mathrm{Categorical}(\omega_{1},\dots,\omega_{K})`$, we get:

$`\displaystyle{\mathbf{Z}}_{{h}}`$$`\displaystyle\leq 8(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+16\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+32\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+32L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{4}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{4}M_{h}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}+8P_{h}^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+4\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{k}\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)}{{C}_{h}^{2}}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+4\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\frac{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{k}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]-\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}},k}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)^{2}}{{C}_{h}^{2}}+\frac{{R}_{h}^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}{d}_{h}C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle=8(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}_{h}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+16\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+32\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}\left[32L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+\frac{2}{C_{h}^{2}}+8P_{h}^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+4\frac{R_{h}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\frac{\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{{}_{k}}\left[\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)\right]+\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{k}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)\right]+\frac{{R}_{h}^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}{d}_{h}C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}.`$

Final bound on loss $`\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t+1)}}\right)\right]`$

We can thus rewrite Equation [E.26](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E26 "Equation E.26 ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") having $`\kappa=\left[1-8(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\right]`$ as

$`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t+1)}}\right)\right]\leq{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)-\frac{\kappa{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+8H\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+16H\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}M_{h}^{2}\left[16L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{C_{h}^{2}}+4P_{h}^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+2\frac{\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{R_{h}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{{}_{k}}\left[\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)\right]+\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{k}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}}{2{\xi}^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{R}_{h}^{2}{d}_{h}.`$

Rearranging and taking an average over all aggregation steps $`t=0,\dots,T-1`$ and having $`\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\star}`$ such that $`{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{\star}}\right)\leq\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{t}}\right)\right]`$, we finally get:

$`\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{2\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(0)}}\right)-{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{\star}}\right)\right]}{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}{T}}+16H\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+32H\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{R}_{h}^{2}{d}_{h}+2\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}M_{h}^{2}\left[16L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{C_{h}^{2}}+4P_{h}^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+4\frac{\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}}{\xi^{2}T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{R_{h}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left[\mathbb{E}_{{}_{k}}\left[\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)\right]+\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{k}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)\right]`$

Per Theorem 1 in abadi2016gaussianmoments and Lemma 1 and Theorem 1 from mcmahan2018learning to guarantee $`(\varepsilon,\delta)`$-privacy $`z^{2}\geq const\frac{q^{2}T\log{1/\delta}}{\varepsilon^{2}}`$, while $`\sigma_{\mathrm{DP}}=z\cdot\mathbb{S}=z\cdot\mathrm{max}_{i=1}^{K}\omega_{i}/q`$. Then to get the final bound with $`(\varepsilon,\delta)`$-privacy guarantee, we must select $`\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{DP}}}^{2}=const\frac{\mathrm{max}_{i=1}^{K}(\omega_{i})^{2}T\ln{\frac{1}{\delta}}}{\varepsilon^{2}}`$.

∎

Remark: For simplicity we assumed that $`\beta_{1}=0`$ and regularizer $`\lambda=0`$ in the $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ optimizer. However, the proof can be extended to the cases with $`\beta_{1}>0`$ and $`\lambda>0`$.

### E.6 Finite-Time Convergence Rates

###### Corollary 1.

Assume [A1.1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex1 "Equation A1.1 ‣ Item 1 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), [A2.1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex3 "Equation A2.1 ‣ Item 2 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), [A2.2](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex4 "Equation A2.2 ‣ Item 2 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), and [A3](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.Ex5 "Equation A3 ‣ Item 3 ‣ E.1 Assumptions ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), $`{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}L<1`$ and $`\kappa=\left[1-8(1-\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})^{2}\right]>0`$. If the trust ratio from Eq. [E.9](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#A5.E9 "Equation E.9 ‣ E.4 LAMB ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") in $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ optimizer is controlled in the Algorithm [1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#alg1 "Algorithm 1 ‣ FL with Differential Privacy (DP) ‣ 2 Federated Learning with Differential Privacy: Background and Notation ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") (global optimizer is $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ and local optimizer is SGD) such that $`{r}_{h}^{(t)}\leq{R}_{h}`$ and $`\left\|{\mathbf{1}}-{\mathbf{p}}_{{h}}^{{(t)}}\right\|_{\infty}\leq{P}_{h}`$, $`\beta_{1}=0`$ and $`\lambda=0`$ in the $`\mathrm{LAMB}`$ optimizer, clients are i.i.d. sampled with probability $`q=1`$ (no sampling), and $`\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{L\sqrt{T}}\right)`$ and $`\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{L\sqrt{T_{\mathrm{loc}}T}}\right)`$, then Algorithm [1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#alg1 "Algorithm 1 ‣ FL with Differential Privacy (DP) ‣ 2 Federated Learning with Differential Privacy: Background and Notation ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") converges to a stationary point of the global loss function with the convergence bound characterized as:

$`\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\Bigg)}_{\text{optimization}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\Bigg(\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}}{T}\Bigg)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\text{global update noise}\end{subarray}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\Bigg(\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}}{T}\Bigg)}_{\text{local update noise}}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(C^{2}{\sigma}_{{}_{\mathrm{DP}}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{R}_{h}^{2}d_{h}\right)}_{\text{differential privacy noise}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\right)}_{\text{clipping bias}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{R_{h}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\left[\Psi_{\mathrm{h}}^{{}^{\mathrm{intra}}}+\Psi_{\mathrm{h}}^{{}^{\mathrm{inter}}}\right]\right)}_{\text{intra and inter-client update variance}},`$

where $`\Psi_{\mathrm{h}}^{{}^{\mathrm{intra}}}=\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t,k}}\left[\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)\right]`$ and $`\Psi_{\mathrm{h}}^{{}^{\mathrm{inter}}}=\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t}}\left[\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{k}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)\right]`$, $`k\sim\mathrm{Categorical}(\omega_{1},\dots,\omega_{K})`$ and $`\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\cdot\right]`$ denotes the expectation over sampled mini-batch $`\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}`$ every local step $`t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=1,\dots,T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}`$ from the client data: $`\boldsymbol{x}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{k}\sim\mathcal{D}_{k}`$, $`\boldsymbol{x}^{(t,t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}_{k}\in\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}`$, $`|\mathscr{B}_{k}^{(t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}})}|=B_{k}`$.

###### Proof.

Using Theorem [2](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#Thmtheorem2 "Theorem 2. ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"), we have

$`\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{2\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(0)}}\right)-{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{\star}}\right)\right]}{{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}{T}}+16H\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+32H\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}^{2}}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{R}_{h}^{2}{d}_{h}+2\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}M_{h}^{2}\left[16L^{2}\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{C_{h}^{2}}+4P_{h}^{2}\right]`$ $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+4\frac{\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}}{\xi^{2}T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{R_{h}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left[\mathbb{E}_{{}_{k}}\left[\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)\right]+\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{k}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)\right].`$

Choosing $`\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}={1}/{\sqrt{T}}`$ and $`\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}={1}/{\sqrt{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}T}}`$, we get $`\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}T=\sqrt{T}`$ and $`\eta_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}{T_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}}/{T}={T_{\mathrm{loc}}}/{T^{2}}`$. Substituting these in the above bound we get

$`\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{2\left[{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(0)}}\right)-{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{\star}}\right)\right]}{\sqrt{T}}+16H\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}+32H\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{R}_{h}^{2}{d}_{h}+2\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}M_{h}^{2}\left[16L^{2}\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T}+\frac{1}{C_{h}^{2}}+4P_{h}^{2}\right]`$$`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{4}{\xi^{2}}\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{R_{h}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left[\mathbb{E}_{{}_{k}}\left[\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left(\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right)\right]+\mathsf{Var}_{{}_{k}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{G}}_{{h,k}}^{{(t)}}\right\|\right]\right)\right].`$

Above can be rewritten as using the big-$`\mathcal{O}`$ and definition of $`\Psi_{\mathrm{h}}^{{}^{\mathrm{intra}}}`$ and $`\Psi_{\mathrm{h}}^{{}^{\mathrm{inter}}}`$ as

$`\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}_{{}_{t_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}}}\left[\left\|\nabla{\mathscr{L}}\left({\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}^{{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}^{2}}{T}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}\sigma_{{}_{\mathrm{loc}}}^{2}}{T}\right)`$$`\displaystyle\qquad+\mathcal{O}\left(C^{2}{\sigma}_{\mathrm{DP}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{R}_{h}^{2}d_{h}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{T_{\mathrm{loc}}}{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{R_{h}^{2}M_{h}^{2}}{C_{h}^{2}}\left[\Psi_{\mathrm{h}}^{{}^{\mathrm{intra}}}+\Psi_{\mathrm{h}}^{{}^{\mathrm{inter}}}\right]\right).`$

∎

### E.7 Recovering Prior Bounds

#### Sublinear Convergence.

Similar to prior works in FL (reddi2021adaptive; azam2021recycling; chen2020understanding; wang2020tackling; karimi2021layer; karimireddy2020scaffold; li2020fedprox) we highlight that Algorithm [1](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#alg1 "Algorithm 1 ‣ FL with Differential Privacy (DP) ‣ 2 Federated Learning with Differential Privacy: Background and Notation ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping") follows the best known convergence rate of $`\mathcal{O}\left(1/\sqrt{T}\right)`$ for non-convex setting. Furthermore, in this section we provide a sketch for recovering the approximate bound for other terms as seen in prior work:

#### Federated Averaging (konecny2015fl; wang2020tackling).

Similar to analysis in reddi2021adaptive (see Remark 1 about Theorem 1 & 2 in reddi2021adaptive), setting $`{\eta}_{{}_{\mathrm{glob}}}=1`$ does not recover the bound in Federated Averaging. However, starting with the final convergence bound of Theorem [2](https://arxiv.org/html/2310.00098v4#Thmtheorem2 "Theorem 2. ‣ E.5 Adaptive Optimizers and Per-Layer Clipping: The Main Proof ‣ Appendix E Theoretical Analysis ‣ Enabling Differentially Private Federated Learning for Speech Recognition: Benchmarks, Adaptive Optimizers and Gradient Clipping"):
